{{Short description|none}}
{{redirect|List of footballers in England and Scotland by number of league appearances|similar list of players in Scotland|List of footballers in Scotland by number of league appearances}}
{{Use dmy dates|date=May 2023}}
The following is a list of the 939 footballers who have made at least 500 domestic league appearances in English league football. This includes the appearances and goals of current and former players in the [[Premier League]] and [[English Football League]].

[[Peter Shilton]] has made the most appearances, and is the only player to have played in over one thousand games. [[Chris O'Grady]] made appearances for 17 different clubs, while 49 players made their appearances [[List of one-club men in association football|for only one team]]. [[Frank Womack]] is the only outfield player who failed to score a goal in his appearances, while [[Arthur Rowley]] scored 433. [[Stanley Matthews]] made his appearances during a career spanning 34 seasons.
